# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-09-30 through 2026-10-06

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-03 — City of Forest Day Nature Tour + Rana Fifteen

**City of Forest Day Nature Tour, then a meal.** Turkish at Rana Fifteen. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10:30 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Prospect+Park&destination=209+4th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/city-of-forest-day-nature-tour/) · [Restaurant](https://www.ranafifteen.com/)

### 2026-09-30 — Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West with New York Swings + Elder

**Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West with New York Swings, then dinner.** British Indian chophouse at Elder. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=385+9th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=450+10th+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10018&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://manhattanwestnyc.com/event/gather-round-lets-dance-2026-2/2026-09-30/) · [Restaurant](https://www.eldernyc.com/)

### 2026-09-30 — Bhangra in the Plaza + Cospa Ramen

**Bhangra in the Plaza, then dinner.** Ramen at Cospa Ramen. It is a 13-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Broadway+and+W+33rd+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=31+East+20th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://34thstreet.org/calendar/event/bhangra-in-the-plaza/2026-09-30) · [Restaurant](https://www.cosparamen.com/)

### 2026-09-30 — Unsinkable: Poems Inspired by the Titanic + La Vara

**Unsinkable: Poems Inspired by the Titanic, then dinner.** Spanish and Sephardic at La Vara. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=213+Water+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10038&destination=268+Clinton+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://southstreetseaportmuseum.org/titanic-poems/) · [Restaurant](https://www.lavarany.com/)

**DUMBO First Thursday Gallery Walk: 2026-27 season opener, then dinner.** Spanish and Sephardic at La Vara. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.

### 2026-09-30 — Mary's Fish Camp + Georgia Gardner Gray: Cookie

**Dinner, then Georgia Gardner Gray: Cookie.** Seafood at Mary's Fish Camp. It is an 11-minute walk from the venue. Georgia Gardner Gray: Cookie starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.

### 2026-10-01 — Hommali + Georgia Gardner Gray: Cookie

**Dinner, then Georgia Gardner Gray: Cookie.** Thai at Hommali. It is an 11-minute walk from the venue. Georgia Gardner Gray: Cookie starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.

#### [Bhangra in the Plaza](https://34thstreet.org/calendar/event/bhangra-in-the-plaza/2026-09-30)

6:00 PM · dance · Free · Greeley Square Plaza · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 75.0  
Free public bhangra class and performance with Sarina Jain and dancers from Masala Bhangra, celebrating contemporary Indian music and dance.
**Timing:** Starts 6pm; Wednesday series continues through October 21
**Practical note:** Outdoor plaza event; wear shoes you can move in.
**Why now:** In-window Wednesday installment of a fall plaza series.
**On-sale watch:** Pre-registration is recommended but not described as mandatory.

#### [Georgia Gardner Gray: Cookie](https://www.thehighline.org/art/projects/georgia-gardner-gray/)

7:30 PM · theater · Free · The High Line, former Weichsel Beef parking lot · Meatpacking District · Manhattan · Score 69.0  
Georgia Gardner Gray's site-specific High Line Performance commission stages a picaresque play about grifters, fake tailors, hunger, and social decay beneath the High Line.
**Timing:** Performance September 30, October 1, and October 2 at 7:30pm; rain date October 4 if needed.
**Practical note:** Outdoor performance; NWS forecast is mostly cloudy with no rain expected Wednesday night.
**Why now:** This is the opening night of a three-night High Line commission.
**Book soon:** RSVP requested; outdoor performance may move to rain date.

MoMath opens the first major exhibition devoted to the mathematics behind M.C. Escher's work, with prints, drawings, woodblocks, and objects exploring symmetry, infinity, and impossible spaces.
**Timing:** Free opening celebration October 1, 6:30-9pm; wait list admitted from 7:30pm as space permits.
**Practical note:** Opening event is listed as full, but MoMath says an on-site wait list may be admitted from 7:30pm.
**Why now:** The exhibition opens October 1 and runs through February 8, 2027.

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.7  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 53.4  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.

## Where to eat

Verified against restaurant-owned menu/event pages and captured source evidence; kept current, sourceable deals, prix-fixe/special menus, recent restaurant openings, and dated events while removing duplicates and unsupported cluster cards.

Dining research: 2026-09-30T10:33:54+00:00

No NYC Michelin-star or Michelin Guide-selection claim was verified for these candidates. El Califa de León’s Michelin note applies to the Mexico City original, not the Flatiron outpost. Kirbee’s source mentions Michelin recognition tied to the founders’ Texas restaurants/people, not Kirbee’s NYC.

### Weekday aperitivo in the bar and lounge

Deals & happy hours — Ci Siamo

Official page lists a dedicated aperitivo menu in the bar and lounge, useful as a pre-dinner Manhattan West option with snacks such as focaccia, gnocco fritto, mortadella and pizzette.

Price: aperitivo menu items checked included $14-$27 snacks; cocktails on drinks menu checked at $19-$22  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 3:00pm-4:30pm; location page also lists lunch daily 11:30am-3:00pm and dinner daily 4:30pm-10:00pm  
Conditions: Available in the bar and lounge Monday-Friday 3:00pm-4:30pm per Ci Siamo’s official aperitivo menu; checked aperitivo items include $14-$27 snacks, with drinks menu cocktails at $19-$22. Tax, tip, service charges and reservation requirement not stated.  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

Deals & happy hours — Hav & Mar

Marcus Samuelsson’s Chelsea seafood restaurant lists a dedicated happy hour on its official location page, useful before gallery/High Line plans.

Schedule: Monday-Friday 5:00pm-7:00pm; dinner Sunday-Saturday 5:00pm-10:00pm  
Conditions: Official location page confirms Happy Hour Monday-Friday 5:00pm-7:00pm and dinner daily 5:00pm-10:00pm; prices and bar-only restrictions were not exposed in checked accessible text.
